## Navigating raw data

Each trial link above resolves to a directory containing `manifest.json` (model, sampling, timing, token usage), `verdict.json` (pass/fail, failure class, grader output), `out/` (adapter logs and `pi_session.jsonl` rollout trace), and for Harbor suites `jobs/` plus `workdir/`.
